## Document 017: 158円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 ～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の“三つの分岐点”が方向性を決める～

Source PDF: all report pdf/158円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 ～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の“三つの分岐点”が方向性を決める～.pdf

Investment Navigator

2026 年 10 月 22 日

1/6 
158 円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 
～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の"三つの分岐点"が方向性を決める～ 
 経済調査部 シニア・フェロー  嶌峰 義清 
（℡：050-5474-7489）

（要旨） 
○ ドル円相場は、7 月末の日米協調介入、9 月上旬の急激な円高、9 月の日米同時利上げを経て、
10 月に入ってからは1 ドル＝158 円前後の狭いレンジでの推移が続いている。円先物市場に
おける非商業部門の建玉は、9 月前半に過去に例のない規模で円の売り越しから買い越しへ
と転じた。しかし、その後は買い越し幅が縮小し、売建玉が再び増え始めている。投機筋の
円買い意欲は弱まりつつあると言えよう。 
○ 期待インフレ率 （BEI） で実質化した日米の実質金利差は、政策金利・2 年債・10 年債のいず
れでも 2％ポイントを超える米国優位が続いている。名目金利差は縮小したものの、米国の
金利上昇は実質金利の上昇、日本のは期待インフレ率の上昇によるところが大きい。このた
め、実質ベースのドル優位は崩れていない。 
○ 当面の焦点は、①11 月 3 日の米中間選挙、②選挙後のイラン情勢、③日銀の 12 月利上げと
その後のペース、の三つである。民主党が上下両院を制し、イラン情勢が沈静化に向かい、
日銀が利上げ路線を明確にすれば、円高方向への転換が視野に入る。逆の組み合わせとなれ
ば、再び160 円台を試し、介入への警戒が強まる展開も否定できない。

1. 投機筋の円ポジションは"円売り一色"から変化し、158 円を挟んで膠着が続くドル円相場 
ドル円相場は、方向感を欠いた推移が続いている。10 月入り後の値動きは、高値が158.94 円
（10 月14 日） 、安値が156.99 円（10 月2 日）と、月間の値幅は2 円弱にとどまる。 
もっとも、ここに至るまでの道のりは平坦ではなかった。4 月末から5 月の大型連休中には、
160 円台後半まで進んだ円安に対して、政府・日銀が総額11.7 兆円の円買い介入を実施した。そ
れでも円安基調は止まらず、7 月 28 日には163.91 円（ECB参照値）と、約40 年ぶりの円安水準を
つけた。これに対し、7 月 31 日には日米当局が28 年ぶりとなる円買い協調介入に踏み切った。介
入額は 15 兆3993 億円に達し、年初からの累計は27 兆 1342 億円と暦年で過去最大を更新した。 
その後は円高へと自律的な反発も見られた。9 月2～3 日には、日銀の追加利上げ観測の強まり
や米雇用関連指標の下振れを受けてドル円は1 日で3 円以上下落、9 日には153 円台まで円高が進
んだ。この局面では介入実績は無く、ジャクソンホール会議でのウォーシュFRB議長の発言をきっ

### Page 2

2/6 
かけに積み上がったドル買い・円売りの持ち高が、損切りを迫られた結果と考えられる。 
ところが、その後は再び円安に押し戻された。FRBは 9 月 16 日に約3 年ぶりの利上げに踏み切
り、政策金利を3.75～4.00％とした。日銀も18 日に政策金利を1.25％へ引き上げたものの、日
米がそろって利上げしたことで金利差は大きく変わらず、ドル円は158 円台へと戻っている。 
為替市場における"投機的な動き"の指標とされるのが、円先物市場における非商業部門（主に
ヘッジファンドなどとされる）の建玉である。その推移を見ると、ここ数ヶ月で大きな変化が生
じていることがわかる（図表1）。 
6 月 2 日時点の円売建玉は24 万 4416 枚と、円キャリートレードがブームだった2007 年以来の
高水準に達していた。差引（ネット）でも12 万9567 枚の売り越しだった。その後も、9 月1 日時
点では 9 万2227 枚の売り越しと、円売り超の状態が続いていた。 
しかし、9 月前半の急激な円高を境に状況は一変し、9 月 15 日時点では差引が12 万 0359 枚の
買い越しへと転じ、わずか2 週間で円買い方向に21 万枚強も振れた。この振れ幅は、統計をさか
のぼれる1986 年以降で最大である。同時点の円買建玉（23 万 7951 枚）も過去最大を記録した。 
もっとも、その後は買い越し幅の縮小が続いている。10 月13 日時点では、買建玉19 万 1256 枚
に対し売建玉14 万 1049 枚で、差引の買い越しは5 万0207 枚とピークの半分以下に縮んだ。売建
玉は 9 月末の12 万枚台から14 万枚台へと再び増えており、円売りの持ち高が積み上がり始めて
いる兆しもうかがえる。投資主体別に見ると、レバレッジド・ファンドは10 月 13 日時点で3 万
3673 枚の円売り越しとなっており、ヘッジファンドの円買い意欲がすでに後退していることを示
している。 
足元の売建玉は、過去の介入局面のように当局が介入を急がなければならない水準にはない。
一方で、9 月に積み上がった円の買い持ちの解消そのものが、円安圧力として作用している面もあ
ると考えられる。

2.  実質金利差から見たドル円相場
為替相場の方向性を最終的に決めるのは、日米の金融政策と、それを反映した金利差である。
そこで、日米の実質金利差を、政策金利・2 年債・10 年債の3 つの年限で見てみる（図表2） 。こ
こでは、実質化に用いる物価として、市場が織り込む期待インフレ率であるBEI（ブレーク・イー
ブン・インフレ率、10 年債）を用いた。足元の消費者物価は、米国では原油高で押し上げられ、
日本では電気・ガス料金支援で押し下げられており、実勢を見誤りやすいためである。
名目で見ると、政策金利は米FF金利の誘導目標が3.75～4.00％、日本の無担保コール翌日物が
1.25％である。10 月 21 日時点の国債利回りは、米国が2 年債4.79％・10 年債 5.13％、日本が2
年債 1.886％・10 年債2.882％となっている。一方、10 年BEIは米国が2.35％（10 月20 日） 、日
本が 2.1～2.2％程度と、ほぼ同水準である。これを差し引いて実質化すると、筆者の試算ではお

おむね次のようになる。
✓ 政策金利：米国＋1.5～1.6％程度、日本▲0.9～1.0％程度（米国優位・差は約2.5％ポイント）
✓ 2 年債：米国＋2.4％程度、日本▲0.3％程度（米国優位・差は約2.7％ポイント）
✓ 10 年債：米国＋2.8％程度、日本＋0.7％程度（米国優位・差は約2.1％ポイント）
名目で見れば、日本の10 年債利回りが9 月下旬に3.115％と約 30 年ぶりの水準に達するなど、
日米金利差は縮小してきた。しかし、BEIで実質化すると景色は異なる。すべての年限で米国の実
質金利が2％ポイント以上も上回っており、ドル優位の構図はほとんど崩れていないのである。
その背景には、日米で金利上昇の"中身"が異なることがある。米国では、昨年末から長期金利
が約 1％ポイント上昇したが、BEIは 2.25％から2.35％へとほぼ横ばいにとどまった。名目金利の
注：BEIで実質化

4/6 
上昇は、ほぼそのまま実質金利の上昇となり、10 年物の実質利回りは9 月末に 2.93％と2008 年
以来の高水準に達した。これに対し日本では、BEIが過去最高圏の2％台まで上昇しており、名目
金利の上昇の相応の部分を期待インフレ率の上昇が占めている。実際、9 月の日銀の利上げ後も
BEIはむしろ上昇した。日本の金利上昇は"インフレ期待による金利上昇"の色彩が濃く、実質金利
の上昇は限られていると言えよう。 
日本の政策金利は、実質では依然として▲1％近いマイナス圏にあり、金融環境はなお緩和的
だ。日銀の利上げや介入にもかかわらず円安の流れが変わらなかったのは、こうした実質金利差
の構図で説明できる面が大きい。逆に言えば、日本の期待インフレ率が2％近辺で落ち着いたまま
利上げが進むか、米国の実質金利が低下に転じない限り、本格的な円高への転換は難しいと考え
られる。 
なお、日本の物価連動国債は発行残高が約10 兆円と小さく、BEIには流動性の影響も含まれ
る。政策金利や2 年債を10 年BEIで実質化するのも簡便的な手法である点には留意が必要だ。

3.  中間選挙の結果次第で変わるドルの行方 
11 月3 日には米国の中間選挙が行われる。1934 年以降の23 回の中間選挙のうち、現職大統領
の政党が上下両院とも議席を増やしたのはわずか2 回しかない。今回も、ロイター通信の世論調
査ではトランプ大統領の支持率が過去最低の31％まで落ち込み、投票先では民主党47％、共和党
35％と 12 ポイントの差がついている。選挙予測サイトのディシジョン・デスクHQは、民主党が下
院を制する確率を78％、上院を制する確率を56％と見ている。 
結果ごとに、ドル円相場への影響を整理すると次のようになる。 
① 民主党が上下両院を制する場合：予算や減税、関税政策に対する議会のチェックが強まり、
財政拡張への期待が後退することで、米長期金利の低下とドル安につながりやすい。FRBの人事に
対する議会の影響力が強まり、金融政策の独立性への懸念が和らぐことも、長期金利の押し下げ
要因となろう。一方で、予算案を巡る対立が先鋭化すれば、政府機関の閉鎖などを通じたリスク
回避の円買いが生じる可能性もある。いずれにしても、ドル円には下押し圧力がかかる公算が大
きい。 
② 下院は民主党、上院は共和党の"ねじれ"となる場合：現在の政策の大枠が維持される一方
で、新たな大型財政出動は難しくなる。相場の反応は限定的で、関心は再び日米の金融政策へと
移ることになろう。 
③ 共和党が両院の過半数を維持する場合：減税や規制緩和など拡張的な政策への期待から、米
長期金利の上昇とドル高が進みやすい。ドル円が再び160 円台を試し、介入への警戒が一気に高
まる展開も想定される。 
世論調査どおりであれば①ないし②の公算が大きく、ドル円にはどちらかと言えば円高寄りの
材料となろう。ただし、重要なのは選挙後の財政・通商政策や金利見通しの変化であり、選挙直
後の値動きだけで方向性を判断するのは早計だろう。

### Page 5

5/6 
4.  イラン情勢：選挙後の"重し"が外れる 
もう一つの大きな不透明要因がイラン情勢である。2 月 28 日に始まった米国・イスラエルとイ
ランの戦闘は、ホルムズ海峡の事実上の封鎖と、米国によるイラン港湾の海上封鎖を伴ったまま
長期化している。北海ブレント原油先物は20 日に一時1 バレル＝111.99 ドルと約5 ヶ月ぶりの高
値をつけた。 
原油高は、いくつかの経路でドル高円安に作用している。米国では、ガソリン価格の前年比
34％の上昇などを背景に、9 月の消費者物価が同＋3.7％へと加速し、FRBの追加利上げ観測を支え
ている。日本にとっては、エネルギー輸入額の増加を通じて貿易収支が悪化し、実需の円売りが
増える。加えて、地政学リスクの高まりは"有事のドル買い"も誘いやすい。 
注目されるのは、トランプ大統領が「中間選挙前にはイランを攻撃しない」と明言している点
だ。言い換えれば、選挙後はこの制約が外れることになる。選挙後に軍事行動が再開されれば、
原油価格の一段高と米利上げ観測の強まりを通じて、ドル高円安が進むリスクがある。逆に、仲
介国を通じた交渉がまとまり、海峡の再開に向かえば、原油価格の下落とともに米長期金利が低
下し、ドル安円高へと振れやすい。実際、20～21 日には原油安を受けて米長期金利が低下し、ド
ルが売られる場面が見られた。

5.  日米の金融政策：12 月の"同時利上げ"の先が焦点 
FRBは9 月に約3 年ぶりの利上げに踏み切り、年内にもう1 回の利上げを示唆した。10 月14 日
に9 月の消費者物価が前年比3.7％と予想を上回ると、27～28 日のFOMCでの利上げ確率は一時5
割前後まで上昇した。しかし、17 日にウォーシュ議長が10 月会合の判断を予断しない姿勢を示し
たことで、利上げ確率は1 割台半ばまで低下している。市場では「10 月は見送り、12 月に利上
げ」との見方が大勢となった。一部には、12 月が今回の利上げ局面で最後になるとの予想も出て
いる。 
一方、日銀の植田総裁は、9 月の利上げ後に「政策の局面は変化した」と述べ、その後も基調的
な物価上昇率が2％に「かなり近づいている」との認識を示した。そのうえで、 「これ以上上がる
ことは容認できない」とも踏み込んでいる。29～30 日の決定会合では政策金利が据え置かれる公
算が大きいが、基調物価が2％に到達したと判断されると報じられている。 
日米がともに12 月に利上げすれば、名目の政策金利差は変わらない。為替にとって重要なの
は、その先の方向性の違いである。FF金利が明確に中立水準を超えるFRBの利上げが最終局面に近
づく一方、日銀は中立金利を目指して利上げを続ける。この構図が鮮明になれば、日米の実質金
利差は徐々に縮小に向かおう。逆に、原油高を受けて米国の物価上昇が加速し、FRBが一段の利上
げを迫られる展開となれば、実質金利差の縮小は見込めず、ドル円相場の膠着状態を演出しよ
う。

6.  今後の見通し：年末に向けて緩やかな円高を想定、ただし分岐は大きい 
以上を踏まえると、当面のドル円相場は、日米の金融政策会合と中間選挙という大きなイベン

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
トを控え、当面は現状の狭いレンジでの推移が続くと考えられる。10 月27～28 日のFOMCは据え置
きが市場の大勢だ。FOMCで利上げが見送られ、その後の日銀会合で基調物価2％到達が確認されて
12 月利上げへの地ならしが進めば、円高方向に振れやすい組み合わせとなる。しかし、市場では
FRBも年内利上げを実施するとの見方が強く、ドル円相場を動かす材料とはなりにくい。逆に、
FRBが10 月のFOMCで利上げを決定した場合、ドル円相場は159 円を超える円安進展の可能性が高
まる。 
年末にかけては、①中間選挙で民主党が議会の主導権を握り、②イラン情勢が交渉による解決
に向かい原油価格が落ち着き、③日銀が12 月に利上げを実施して来年以降も利上げ継続の姿勢を
示す、という三つの条件がそろえば、日米の実質金利差は縮小に向かう。その場合は、150 円台半
ばから前半への円高が進む可能性がある。投機筋の売建玉が再び積み上がっている局面であれ
ば、その巻き戻しが円高の勢いを増幅することも考えられる。 
一方で、①共和党の善戦、②選挙後のイランへの軍事行動の再開、③FRBの利上げ継続＋日銀の
利上げ慎重姿勢打ち出し、といった組み合わせとなれば、ドル円は再び160 円台を試すことにな
ろう。その場合、政府・日銀は過去の介入水準である160 円台前半から164 円近辺を意識しつ
つ、円安のスピードを抑えるための介入に踏み切る公算が大きい。ただし、これまでの経験が示
すように、ファンダメンタルズが変わらない中での介入は"時間を買う"政策にすぎず、効果が一
時的にとどまる可能性には注意が必要だ。 
筆者は、①米中間選挙は最低でも上下院のねじれ、場合によっては上院においても民主党の過
半数獲得、②選挙後に米国がイランへの軍事行動を再開、同時に仲介国を介した交渉を再開する
ものの、膠着状態が続く、③日米とも利上げ継続、をメインシナリオとしている。 
さらにリスク要因として、①米中間選挙の結果がなかなか確定せず、議会勢力の確定に時間が
かかる、②イラン情勢はフーシ派から攻撃を受けているサウジアラビアや、総選挙を終えたイス
ラエルによる本格的な攻撃参戦、の可能性を挙げる。 
このように不透明要因が多いことも足元のドル円相場の膠着の要因になっていると考えられる
が、短期的な値動きに一喜一憂するよりも、実質金利差という基本的な決定要因がどちらに動い
ているかを冷静に見極めることが重要であり、日米中央銀行の金融政策姿勢が最も注目される。

以 上

台玻 3Q26 毛 利 率 攀 至 31.60% ， 優 於 預 期 ：  
台玻 3Q26 營收 141.34 億元， QoQ+17.17% 、 YoY+31.76% ；營業毛利 44.67
億 元 ，QoQ+58.74% 、 YoY+257.07% ， 毛 利 率 約 31.60% ； 營 業 利 益 33.84 億
元， QoQ+121.90% 、YoY+2,186.48% ；稅 後 淨 利 27.05 億 元，QoQ+113.66% 、
YoY+876.53% ， 稅 後 EPS 0.93 元。  
研 究 部 評 析 ：  
台玻 3Q26 受惠 於 Low Dk2 、Low CTE 等 高 毛 利 T-glass 產品 出 貨 比 重 拉 升，
Low Dk1 占比 持 穩 ， 帶 動 3Q26 營 業 毛 利 QoQ+58.74% 、 YoY+257.07% ，且
毛利率較 2Q26 提升 8.27% 至 31.60% ， 亦 優 於 原 預 期 之 26.23% 。 因 此 3Q26
稅後淨利 27.05 億 元 、 稅 後 EPS 0.93 元， 均 優 於 原 預 期 之 20.47 億 元 、0.70
元。 1~3Q26 台 玻 累 計 營 收 達 364.40 億 元 ， YoY+18.69% ， 毛 利 率 25.38% ，
稅後淨利 45.65 億 元 ， YoY 由 虧 轉 盈 ， 稅 後 EPS 1.57 元。  
台玻 為 因應 供 應 鏈 對 於高 階 玻 纖 布之 強 勁 需 求，擬 增 建 及 冷 修 窯 爐，以 擴 充
產 能 ，並 提 升 市 占 率。11/02/2026 董 事 會 通 過 ，桃 園 廠 、 鹿 港 廠 窯 爐 增 建 及
子 公 司 台 嘉 蚌 埠 玻 璃 纖 維 有 限 公 司 冷 修擴 充 案 ， 預 估 投 入 金 額 約新台幣 16.3
億 元 及 人 民 幣 11.5 億 元 。  
展望 2027 ~2028 年， Low CTE 當前 出 貨 量 已 較 2026 年 初 顯 著 增 加 ， 預 期 營
收 佔 比 將 於 2027 年 陸 續 提 升 ， 並 於 2028 年 伴 隨 近 年 陸 續 擴 建 之 新 產 能 開
出 後 ，追趕 Low Dk2 。 研 究 部預期 2027 年 Low Dk2 營 收 比 重 仍 將 高 於 Low 
Dk1 及 Low CTE 。目 前 台 玻 高 階 玻 纖 布訂 單 能 見 度已達 2027 年，且 2028 年
訂 單 洽 談 順 暢 。 若 供 應 鏈 產 品 需 求無大 規 模 質 變 ， 台玻 2028 年 玻 纖 布 事 業
群 仍 將 持 續 成 長 。研 究 部 預 估 台 玻 4Q26 底 ~2027 年 玻 纖 布 營 收 比 重 有 望 超
越 平 板 玻 璃 和 玻 璃 器 皿 。 預 估 2027 /2028 年稅後 EPS 分別為 4.37 /6.15 元 。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

日本：7月以降の設備投資需要はAI関連産業を中心に旺盛
3
 7～9月期の機械受注は増勢回復へ。AI関連業種からの受注がけん引
― ①旺盛なAI需要のもと、関連業種では設備不足感が強まっていたこと、②輸出増が持続するもとで、好業績が期待され
ること、③中東情勢の不確実性が後退したことなどが背景
 先行き設備投資は緩やかな増勢を辿る見込み
― 半導体製造装置をはじめ、AI・データセンター関連品目を中心に受注残が増加しており、目先こうした傾向が続く見通し
― 積み上がった受注残を段階的に消化することで、設備投資が持続的に下支えされる公算が大きい

（注） 季節調整値。最終需要・中間需要物価指数（国内品資本財）を用いて実質化。
左図の26年7～9月期（破線部）は内閣府見通しを基にした推計値。
右図の26年7～9月期は、同7～8月平均の数値

― ヘッジファンドの総資産は2013年の約4兆ドルから2026年初には約13兆ドルに拡大。平時には市場の流動性や効率性

欧州：EU・中国はハイブリッド車の輸入制限で暫定合意。同様の措置は他の品目に広がる可能性
11
 EUと中国は、貿易不均衡の是正措置について暫定合意。中国からのハイブリッド車輸入は従来見通しの半分以下に
―EUが中国産EVに追加関税を賦課した2024年以降、追加関税の対象とならないハイブリッド車輸入が急増
― 欧州委員会の試算では、今回の措置によって2030年までの4年間で数百万台の輸入を削減見込み。直近では、EUが輸
入するハイブリッド車の約半分を中国車が占めており、域内自動車市場に一定のインパクト
 欧州委員会は、幅広い産業で貿易不均衡を是正する必要性を示唆。同様の措置が他の品目にも広がる可能性
― 欧州委員会のモニタリング対象（輸入増加と価格下落が併存する品目）は、中国からの輸入品が最多

 ASEAN5では石油の国家備蓄制度や設備が未整備。石油の安定供給に向けた対応の強化が急務
―ASEANの場合、石油備蓄量は1～2カ月分と限定的。緊急時を見据えた石油備蓄の整備は途上
―10月のAZEC閣僚会合においては、ASEANの石油備蓄拡大や、緊急時の共同備蓄放出に向けた計画などで合意
 電力については域内での需要拡大が見込まれる中、発電能力の増強や送配電網の整備による電力供給の拡大が課題
―IEAは東南アジアの電力需要が2030年まで年平均5.4％増加すると予測。産業や冷房、データセンター需要が押し上げ
― 再エネ含む多様な電源による供給拡大や、AEBFでも議論された国際送配電網の整備や電力融通の取り組みが主眼に

日本：コメ価格下落で企業物価は鈍化。もっとも、先行きは原油高を背景に再加速へ

 9月の国内企業物価はコメ価格の下落を主因に、前年比＋6.8％（前月：同＋7.6％）と鈍化
― 新米価格が昨年を下回ったこと等を背景に、コメ価格が大幅に下落。国内企業物価を▲0.75%Pt押し下げ
― 一方、3月来の原油高を背景に化学・プラスチック製品は高止まり。また、AI需要等を背景に非鉄金属も高い伸びを維持
 7月以降の原油高再燃を背景に、先行きの国内企業物価は再加速する見込み
― 原油価格高止まりにタンカー運賃の急騰が重なり、先行きの輸入物価は原油関連を中心に加速へ
― 国内企業物価は石油・石炭製品やラグを伴いつつ化学・プラスチック製品などを中心に再加速する見込み

◼ TOPIX 啟動大規模改革：日本交易所集團 10 月 7 日公布 TOPIX 調整結果，符合新制納入標
準的成分股將由 9 月底的 1,636 檔降至 986 檔，另有 35 檔股票新納入。現有成分股中，共
有 683 檔未符合流動性及浮動市值等標準，這些股票將自 2026 年 10 月底起逐步降低權
重，並於 2027 年 10 月重新評估，屆時仍未達標者將於 2028 年 7 月底降至零。此次改革有
助於改善 TOPIX 成分股的流動性及投資價值，並促使企業更加重視市場評價。

◼ 巴西總統選舉波索納洛首輪領先魯拉：巴西最高選舉法院 10 月 5 日公布首輪投票結果，參
議員弗拉維奧・波索納洛取得 47.03%的有效票，現任總統魯拉獲得 45.16%，兩人均未取得
過半票數，將於 10 月 25 日進行第二輪決選。若最終由波索納洛當選將相較有利於巴西股市
表現。
◼ IEA 支持加速釋出既有儲油：國際能源署（IEA）10 月 7 日表示，會員國支持加快釋出 3 月
已承諾提供的儲油，並將優先增加柴油供應。截至目前，會員國已釋出約 3.25 億桶儲油；若
剩餘承諾全數執行，可再向市場提供約 1 億桶供應，有助緩解運輸業與農業面臨的燃料成本
壓力。隨各國持續釋出戰略儲備石油，將有利於在短期內壓抑全球油價進一步上行。
市場展望
◼ 標普 500 獲利預估連續上修，資訊科技為主要推升來源：標普 500 企業獲利預估連續兩季上
修，其中資訊科技產業為主要改善來源，反映 AI、雲端運算及資料中心需求仍然強勁。雖然
美國資料中心仍面臨電力與基礎設施瓶頸，但建置規模持續擴大，AI 投資也逐步延伸至記憶

體、網路設備、資安及企業應用等領域。因此，預期科技類股的獲利動能仍具支撐，具 AI 供
應鏈優勢的台股與日股亦可望延續相關成長動能。
◼ 中天期債券可望兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性，加上政府與企業發債供給維持高
檔，可能使市場利率維持在較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企業獲利、
現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，較高的美國公債基準利率也使其利息收益維持一定水準。短天
期債券面臨利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較易受到公債供給及利率波動影響，因
此中天期美國公債及投資級債可望在息收與利率風險之間維持較為平衡的表現。

2026年 10月 30日 全8頁

Indicators Update 
2026年 9月雇用統計 
失業率は2.4％と前月から0.1％pt低下し、就業拡大が進展

経済調査部 エコノミスト 菊池 慈陽

[要約] 
◼ 2026年 9月の完全失業率（季節調整値）は 2.4％と、2カ月ぶりに低下（前月差▲
0.1％pt）し、7月の水準に戻った。失業者数は「自発的な離職」を中心に 2カ月ぶり
に減少（同▲10万人）し、就業者数は2カ月連続で増加（同＋34万人）した。労働参
加と就業拡大が進展し、雇用環境は前月から改善したとみられる。 
◼ 2026年9月の有効求人倍率（季節調整値）は1.19倍（前月差＋0.01pt）と3カ月ぶり
に上昇した。新規求職申込件数が大きく減少したことで、新規求人倍率は2.19倍（同
＋0.13pt）と上昇した。 
◼ 先行きの雇用環境は堅調に推移しよう。労働供給が中長期的に減少していく可能性が
高いこともあり、企業は高水準の賃上げなど、人材確保に向けた積極的な取り組みを
続けている。ただし、雇用環境の悪化リスクは小さくない。中東情勢をめぐる不確実
性は依然として高く、長期化する原油高が企業収益を悪化させ、幅広い産業で労働需
要を下押しする恐れがある。

9月の完全失業率：2.4％と前月から低下、「自発的な離職」による失業者が大きく減少 
2026年 9月の完全失業率（季節調整値）は2.4％と、2カ月ぶりに低下（前月差▲0.1％pt）
し、7月の水準に戻った（図表2左上） 。失業者数は2カ月ぶりに減少（同▲10万人）し、就業
者数は 2カ月連続で増加（同＋34万人）した。労働参加と就業拡大が進展し、雇用環境は前月
から改善したとみられる。 
失業者数を求職理由別に見ると、「自発的な離職」 （前月差▲13万人）が大きく減少し、全体
を押し下げた。「勤め先や事業の都合による離職」（同▲1万人）は小幅に減少した。他方、「定
年又は雇用契約の満了」（同＋1万人）と「新たに求職」（同＋1万人）は小幅に増加した（巻末
の雇用概況①下段左）。 
就業者数の前月からの変化を業種別に見ると、「製造業」と「医療，福祉」が大きく増加し、
全体を押し上げた。「卸売業，小売業」と「情報通信業」も増加した。他方、「建設業」やその
他の非製造業は減少した。（図表2左下）。 
雇用者数（役員を除く）を雇用形態別に見ると、正規雇用者は前月から 22万人増加した。
2026年春頃から増加傾向にあり、ピークだった2025年 11月の水準を上回った。非正規雇用者
は 5カ月ぶりに増加（前月差＋21万人）した（図表 2右下）。 
図表 2：就業者数・完全失業率（左上） 、非労働力人口・労働参加率（右上） 、

9月の新規求人倍率：求職が大きく減少した一方で求人は増加し、前月から上昇 
2026年 9月の有効求人倍率（季節調整値）は 1.19倍（前月差＋0.01pt）と 3カ月ぶりに上
昇した。新規求人倍率は2.19倍（同＋0.13pt）と上昇した（図表3左）。 
求人側の動きを見ると、有効求人数は前月から横ばいだった一方、新規求人数（前月比＋
1.5％）は 2カ月ぶりに増加した（図表 4左） 。求職側では、有効求職者数（同▲1.4％）、新規
求職申込件数（同▲4.4％）はいずれも 3カ月ぶりに減少した。求職側は 2025年春頃から持ち
直しの動きが見られており、求人側も2026年春頃から増加傾向にある。 
雇用形態別に見ると、正社員の有効求人倍率（季節調整値）は1.01倍（前月差＋0.01pt）と
3カ月ぶりに上昇した。新規求人倍率も 1.79倍（同＋0.11pt）と 3カ月ぶりに上昇した（図表
3右）。

先行き：堅調な推移を見込むも、中東情勢など雇用環境の悪化リスクは小さくない 
先行きの雇用環境は、堅調に推移することを見込んでいる。労働供給が中長期的に減少して
いく可能性が高いこともあり、企業は高水準の賃上げなど1、人材確保に向けた積極的な取り組
みを続けている。 
日本銀行「全国企業短期経済観測調査」 （日銀短観）の2026年 9月調査では、雇用人員判断
DI（全規模全産業、 「過剰」－「不足」 ）は「最近」が▲38％ptと 6月調査から 1％pt低下し
（人手不足感が強まり）、「先行き」は▲41％ptと更なる低下が予想されている（巻末の雇用概
況①上段右）。特に、中小企業（全産業）は「最近」が▲42％ptと6月調査から3％pt低下し、
「先行き」は▲45％ptだった。中小企業を中心に、人手不足感が強まっているとみられる。 
株式会社ナウキャストによる「HRog賃金 Now」の求人数指数を見ると、正社員とパート・ア
ルバイトのいずれも 2026年春頃から上昇傾向にある（巻末の雇用概況③下段左）。人手不足感
の強まりを受けて、今後も労働需要は堅調に推移するとみられる。 
他方、トランプ米政権による高関税政策（トランプ関税）1F
2や、長期化する日中関係の冷え込
みは、引き続き労働需要の下押し要因となるだろう。 
また、雇用環境の悪化リスクは小さくない。中東情勢は依然として不確実性が高い状況が続
いており、とりわけ注意が必要だ。原油等の供給網の正常化は当面見込みにくく、輸入原油価
格が高止まりしている。原油高は原材料費や物流費などの上昇を通じて企業収益を悪化させ3、
幅広い産業で労働需要を下押しする可能性がある。

1 日本労働組合総連合会（連合）が公表した第 7回（最終）回答集計結果では、定期昇給相当込みの賃上げ率
（加重平均）が企業規模計で 5.01％と、前年（5.25％）をやや下回るものの、高水準を維持している。300人
未満の中小組合では 4.69％と前年（4.65％）をわずかながら上回り、中小企業の積極的な姿勢が目立った。詳
細は、日本労働組合総連合会（連合）「3年連続の 5％超え！～2026春季生活闘争 第7回（最終）回答集計結
果について～」（2026年7月3日）を参照。 
2 トランプ米政権は通商法 122条に基づく措置が失効する 7月 24日に新たな関税措置を発動した（日本経済新
聞 電子版「トランプ政権が新関税発動、日本 12.5％ 中間選挙へ看板政策継続」（2026年7月 24日））。 
3 原油高による物価の上昇については、神田慶司・畑中宏仁・中村華奈子・横田凱「日本経済見通し：2026年
8月」（大和総研レポート、2026年8月 24日）を参照。

Airtac
Valuation
We derive our target price of NT$1,430 for Airtac by using a P/E methodology, as we believe the margin and earnings growth
outlooks will be the key drivers of the stock. We apply a P/E of 21.5x for 2027E, which is at par with its historical average.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the Airtac stock from reaching our target price include: 1) a softer-than-expected China
macro economy, resulting in downside to our sales forecasts; 2) intensifying competition leading to gross margin pressures;

and 3) slower-than-expected progress of its linear guide business, causing low utilization of capacity.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

## Document 111: 261103_daiwa_ Fositek

Source PDF: all report pdf/261103_daiwa_ Fositek.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We attended Fositek’s investor meeting following its 3Q26 
results announcement on 3 November. We believe the company will benefit 
from the AI server trend with a rising liquid-cooling penetration rate in the 
following years. With rising server revenue over 2026-28E given its share-
gain potential, we reaffirm our Buy (1) rating. See our previous memo 3Q26 
revenue largely in line with expectations, 7 October 2026.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results beat our estimates and Bloomberg 
consensus. Fositek’s 3Q26 revenue of TWD4,502m (+20.4% QoQ, 
+26.2% YoY), a record high, was in line with our estimate and the 
Bloomberg consensus. Its 3Q26 gross margin came in at 38.0% (vs. 34.0% 
in 2Q26 and 27.7% in 3Q25), also a record-high level, above our estimates 
(34.3%) and the consensus (36.5%), mainly on manufacturing process 
improvements and better-than-expected product mix (server revenue 
accounted for 81.6% in 3Q26, up from 54.9% in 2Q26 and 56.4% in 1Q26). 
Its operating margin of 32.4% (vs. 27.9% in 2Q26 and 22.2% in 3Q25), 
another record high, beat our estimate (28.9%) and the consensus 
(31.2%), on better opex control. Thus, its operating profit of TWD1,460m 
(+39.9% QoQ, +84.2% YoY) beat our estimate and the consensus by 
11.0% and 3.3%, respectively. Its net profit of TWD1,242m (+46.3% QoQ, 
+80.8% YoY) beat our estimate and the consensus by 15.9% and 6.5%, 
respectively. Its 3Q26 EPS finished at TWD18.11. Its 3Q26 product mix 
was: server (81.6%, +92.7% YoY), foldable phone (15.4%, -53.1% YoY), 
notebook (4.5%, -28.1% YoY) and others (0.6%, -10.8% YoY).

Brighter 2027 outlook, driven by server segment. Following the ramping 
up of GPU and ASICs projects in 2H26, we expect a stronger 2027 revenue 
outlook vs. 2026, along with gross margin expansion. With the rise in the 
liquid-cooling penetration rate, quick disconnect (QD) shipments per rack 
and QD spec upgrade for ASP expansion, we forecast its revenue to grow 
by 40.3% YoY in 2026 and 59.2% YoY in 2027. We raise our 2026-28E 
EPS by 9-29% on our more aggressive revenue and gross- and operating-
margin assumptions.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and lift our 12M TP to 
TWD2,910 (from TWD2,615), still based on a target PER of 32x (vs. its 
past-3-year range of 9-42x [average of 24x]), translating to a 0.6x PEG 
over 2026-28E on our 1-year forward EPS. Key downside risks: lower-than-
expected market share for key clients; slower-than-expected penetration 
rate of foldable phones; and geopolitical risks.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 8% above the Bloomberg consensus 
likely as we are more aggressive on revenue.

3 November 2026

Brighter 2027 outlook driven by server segment

➢ 3Q26 results beat our estimates and Bloomberg consensus 
➢ Stronger revenue outlook in 2027 with gross margin expansion  
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating and raising 12M TP to TWD2,910

Company profile 
Fositek is a provider of foldable phone hinges, foldable laptop/tablet hinges, AR/VR glasses hinges, 
ultra-thin laptop hinges and keyboard hinges, as well as hinges for medical instruments and automobile 
applications. Its major clients include Huawei and Motorola.

Fositek Corp (6805 TT): 3 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 7 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
The CEO and chairperson of Fositek are not the same individual, and we see no issue with its
board quality. Besides, we note that the chairperson has 24 years of experience in this industry
and took over the role of chairman at Fositek in 2014, which is a positive sign from a governance
perspective.
Capital management 2 Fositek's dividend payout ratio was 40-60% over 2022-25, due likely to its capacity expansion plans.
Related party &
transaction 2 Revenue generated from related-party transactions (mainly for AVC and SZFSD) was 0-16% of total revenue during 2022-25, and we found no suspicious activity.
E Supply chain management 1
Fositek maintains smooth communication channels with its suppliers and executes its business
operations effectively. In response to ESG issues, Fositek actively implements supply chain
management, striving to optimise the overall environment through product design, raw-material
procurement, manufacturing, transportation, packaging and recycling processes. It integrates
upstream and downstream suppliers to establish a green supply chain. For example, Fositek has
required its suppliers to sign environmental protection agreements since 2021, committing to
comply with hazardous substance control regulations.
G Data security 2
To protect company information and customer data, Fositek has passed the IEC/ISO 27001
information security management system certification and will continue to enhance execution in
various management areas.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

## Document 133: 261105_daiwa_nypcb

Source PDF: all report pdf/261105_daiwa_nypcb.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: NYPCB released its 3Q26 results after market hours on 5 November. Net profit 
was TWD3.30bn (EPS: TWD5.10), 9% below our estimate and the Bloomberg consensus. 
Revenue rose 21% QoQ and 49% YoY to TWD16.38bn, in line with expectations. GM was 
29.3%, 1.0ppt below our estimate and 0.7ppt below consensus, reflecting yield losses 
during the Kunshan ramp-up and a more modest BT substrate margin uplift than we had 
assumed. OPM was 26.4%, versus our 27.5% estimate and 26.5% consensus. Despite 
operating profit being broadly in line, net profit was weighed down by a higher tax rate 
(26% vs. our 22% estimate), reflecting increased profitability at NYPCB’s China factories.

Looking ahead, we expect modest BT and c.10% ABF price hikes in 4Q26, which would 
result in mid-teens % QoQ growth in 4Q26E. This is consistent with its October revenue of 
TWD6.29bn (released 2 November, up 13% MoM and 70% YoY), equivalent to 34% of our 
4Q26 forecast (historic range being 33-39%). Per management, the Kunshan plant’s UTR 
has increased to 90-95% in October (vs. 80-85% in 3Q26 and 65-70% in 2Q26). As 
NYPCB is unwilling to accept LTAs with end clients (compared with its competitors), we 
believe its bargaining power should be the highest among TW substrate vendors. We have 
a Buy (1) rating on NYPCB, with a 12-month TP of TWD2,460, based on a target PER of 
49x applied to our 1-year forward EPS forecast. Key downside risk: weaker-than-expected 
demand for switches.

What’s the impact: 
Below is our first take on NYPCB’s 3Q26 results, released on 5 November.

• 3Q26 net profit missed expectations. Net profit came in at TWD3.30bn (EPS: 
TWD5.10), 9% below both our estimate (TWD3.63bn) and the Bloomberg consensus 
(TWD3.62bn). 3Q26 revenue of TWD16.38bn (up 21% QoQ and 49% YoY) was in-line 
with expectations, while the gross margin reached 29.3%, 1ppt/0.7ppt below 
our/Bloomberg consensus estimates due to loss on the yield rate as the Kunshan plant 
ramped up; our gross margin expansion assumption for BT substrate was also too 
bullish. Operating margin reached 26.4%, versus our 27.5% estimate and the street’s 
26.5%, mainly on the lower-than-expected gross margin. However, while the operating 
profit was largely on track with expectations, net profit was dragged down by a higher-
than-expected tax rate (26% vs. our 22% estimate) due to rising profitability from 
factories in China.

• Business outlook. Looking ahead, we expect modest BT and c.10% ABF price hikes in

33-39%). Per management, the Kunshan plant’s UTR has increased to 90-95% in

should be the highest among TW substrate vendors.

Nan Ya PCB  | Asia Pacific
In-line 3Q26 gross margin
3Q26 results:
• 3Q revenue was NT$16.4bn (+21% q/q, +49% y/y).
• GM reached 29.3% (+460bps q/q, +1,970bps y/y), slightly above our estimate
but in line with the Street, driven by higher ABF (low 30s  %) and BT GM
(high 20s  %).  PCB remains  loss-making on the gross margin level.
• OP was NT$4.3bn (+49% q/q, +597% y/y), 1% above our estimate and the
Street, for OpM of 26.4% (+500bps q/q, +2,070bps y/y).

• However, non-op income was below our estimate and the Street. Tax rate of
~26% was again higher than our estimate at 16% and the Street at 19%.
• Thus, NP came in at NT$3.3bn (+46% q/q, +355% y/y), 13% below our
estimate/8% below the Street, for EPS of  NT$5.10.

Key Takeaways
NYPCB's 3Q GM is  largely in line with both buy-side and sell-side estimates.
The 500bps sequential improvement in OpM looks positive to us. Similar to

Unimicron's 3Q results, we view NYPCB's slight miss as an expectation
mismatch...
...rather than reflecting any structural concerns.
We also expect NYPCB to gain share for ABF substrates for AMD's server CPUs,
as one Japanese competitor reallocates more capacity to another CPU customer.
We remain constructive on all ABF substrate stocks in our coverage.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 14%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 161: 261108_ms_alchip

Source PDF: all report pdf/261108_ms_alchip.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-08

Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

M
Idea
Alchip Technologies Ltd  | Asia Pacific
Second big customer won, with
revenue upside from major
customer; OW
Alchip confirmed winning a second big customer from the US.
Given strong demand from a major customer, it is possible for
Alchip's 2027 revenue to double from 2026 and double again in
2028. We think the stock should react positively to the new
customer win and better 2027-28 outlook.
Major surprise – winning a second customer: Previously, in Alchip Technologies

Ltd: Solid 2027 and 2028 outlook with potential catalysts; OW (11 Oct 2026), we
highlighted that we believe Alchip is a leading candidate for Google’s TPU KGD back-
end design opportunity. While that remains the case, Alchip’s pursuit of another US
HPC customer opportunity has borne fruit earlier. Based on management's
comments, we believe this second customer is one of the major US CSPs; it could
contribute US$1bn revenue in 2027.
Updates on major customer (AWS): Alchip management expect 4Q26 revenue to

trend higher vs. 3Q26. When answering questions regarding AI demand uncertainty,
Alchip doesn’t see any signs of slowdown from the customer side and expects
revenue from its major project (3nm AWS Trainium3) to grow >100% in 2027.
Meanwhile, the next-generation project (2nm AWS Trainium4) will tape out soon;
there should be a small revenue contribution from this in 4Q27. The company
continues to believe that it is the only design service provider for the 2nm project,

given the value it adds for the customer in terms of time to market.
We raise our revenue forecast: We update our revenue breakdown model. We
think 2026 revenue should end up at ~US$2.3bn-US$2.4bn. Management thinks our
US$4.5bn to US$5.0bn revenue outlook for 2027  (doubling from 2026) is
reasonable. We think the company can further double its revenue in 2028, thanks to
AWS Trainium4 production, and revenue streams from the second and third US CSP

Stronger revenue   guidance for 2027 and 2028
New customer contribution: We raised our 2027 and 2028 revenue forecasts given the
second major customer's design win confirmation. The company guided that the
contribution could start from 2Q27 and that revenue scale could reach US$1bn. We
currently expect revenue from this customer to increase a further 20% in 2028 with a
full-year contribution.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Earnings estimate revisions
We cut our 2026 earnings estimate 20%; 2027e and 2028e EPS virtually unchanged:
The cut to 2026 revenue and                  earnings forecasts is mostly driven by the revenue
recognition deferred to 2027 and lower 3Q26 gross margin, which slightly missed the
company's 20% target. We raise 2027-28 revenue forecasts given the new customer
design win, but also lower our margin assumptions for both years –  we think this new

project would also carry a dilutive margin profile because the customer could be a major
US CSP or HPC. We also slightly lower our non-US CSP customer revenue contribution
forecasts for both 2027/2028 as we believe the company will prioritize foundry capacity
for CSP customers. Net,  our 2027 and 2028 EPS estimates change <1%.

4
Price target remains unchanged at NT$5,888
We continue to derive our base case value, which is also our price target, from a residual
income model. Our cost of equity of 10.4% (beta of 1.4, equity risk premium of 6.0%, and
risk-free rate of 2.0%) remains unchanged, as do our intermediate growth rate of  12.5%
and terminal growth rate of 4%.
Our bear case value of NT$2,465 and bull case value of NT$6,880 are also unchanged.

Risk Reward - Alchip Technologies Ltd (3661.TW)
NT$5,888.00
Base case value, derived from a residual income model. We assume the cost of equity is
constant at 10.4% (2% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.4 beta). Our intermediate growth
rate and terminal growth rates are 12.5% and 4%, respectively.
Morgan Stanley EstimatesMean
Consensus Price Target Distribution NT$3,755.00 NT$6,800.00
Source: Reﬁnitiv, Morgan Stanley Research

MS Rating
▪ We continue to believe that AI ASICs will
outgrow AI GPUs in the coming years, and
Alchip is one of the few ASIC design service
providers that can deal with increasingly
complex designs (at 2nm) with good
production/design track records for large
leading edge chips.
▪ AWS's announcement of its 3nm
Trainium3 chip matches the timing of
Alchip's 3nm project win. The growing
number of use cases for Trainium (e.g.,
Apple's adoption) bodes well for Alchip's

revenue growth in 2026.
▪ We believe Alchip has strong potential to
win Trainium4.
▪ Our price target implies 26x our 2027e
EPS, which we ﬁnd undemanding vs. our
>100% revenue CAGR for 2025-28e.
Consensus Rating Distribution
94% Overweight
6% Equal-weight
0% Underweight

Risk Reward Themes
New Data Era: Positive
Secular Growth: Positive
View descriptions of Risk Rewards Themes here
NT$6,880.00
30x 2027e EPS

AI chipsets and HPC projects start to
proliferate, amid substantial end demand.
Alchip's leading-edge tape-out numbers
increase signiﬁcantly, with some high-proﬁle
project wins at 3nm or even 2nm. Alchip is
able to secure enough foundry capacity
(front-end wafers and back-end packaging)
and substrates for all of its leading-edge
turnkey production. Alchip gradually gains
market share within the design service
market, amid continued proliferation of

custom chip demand.
NT$5,888.00
26x 2027e EPS
AI chipsets and HPC projects start to
proliferate amid strong end demand,
resulting in steady growth in tape-outs and
volume. There might be a slowdown in
Alchip's Chinese customers' design activity
for leading-edge chips as a result of US
export controls; we believe this would be
largely offset by ASIC demand strength
from non-China regions.
NT$2,465.00
11x 2027e EPS
AI chipsets and HPC projects lack concrete

end demand, resulting in delayed tape-outs
and mass production. Alchip's leading-edge
project pipeline deteriorates, or some
customers turn directly to TSMC. AI GPU's
dominance in AI computing compresses the
market share gain potential for overall ASIC,
with customers starting to slow their
custom silicon developments - and therefore
ASIC procurement plans.
Risk Reward – Alchip Technologies Ltd (3661.TW)
Strong Trainium4 and second customer's upside in 2028

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.16  
4분기 반등 기대

3Q26 Preview 
현대차는 3Q26E 매출액 42.4조원(-9.4% YoY, -13.9% QoQ), 영업이익 2.5조원(-
2.8% YoY, -13.5% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 각 부문
별 영업이익은 자동차 1.7조원(-2.0% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 4.8%), 금융 5,501억
원(-4.5% YoY, -27.5% QoQ, OPM 6.9%)으로 예상한다. 
생산 정상화 및 신차 확대를 통한 수익성 개선 기대 
현대차는 글로벌 도매 판매 91.8만대(-11.5% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 90.9
만대(-9.6%)를 기록했다. 7~8월에 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생산 차
질이 발생했다. 파업 영향으로 HEV 판매 증가율이 1%에 그치면서, HEV 판매 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선 효과도 시장 기대에 미치지 못했다.  
4분기부터는 3분기 파업 영향으로 지연된 생산량 회복 및 신차 확대가 본격화되면서, 
전분기 대비 매출 및 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 동사는 보급형 전기차 아이오닉3의 튀
르키예 생산을 9월부터 시작했으며, 이를 통해 유럽 시장 판매량은 점차 회복될 전망
이다. 또한, 1) HMGMA를 통한 HEV 미국 현지 생산 확대, 2) 제네시스 HEV 모델 순
차적 출시를 통해 내년부터 북미를 중심으로 믹스 개선이 본격화될 전망이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 520,000원으로 하향 
2027년 영업이익은 11.2조원(+10.2%)를 기록할 것으로 예상된다. 원화 약세 지속을 
반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다. 다만, 현재 주가는 
12M Fwd P/E 기준 6.1배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 수준이다. 1) RMAC 
성과 구체화, 2) RA(로봇 생산법인) 착공 등 Physical AI 사업이 구체화될 경우 밸류에
이션 할증이 가능할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr

## Document 225: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성전자 (005930)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정  
3Q26E 영업이익 106.1조원, 원화강세 불구 견조한 수준 기록 전망 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익은 106.1조원으로 낮아진 시장 기대치 (104조원)를 소폭 
능가할 전망이다. 해당 분기동안 급격한 원화강세로 인해 실적 측면의 비우호적 환경
이 조성되었지만, 동사는 강건한 판매 정책에 기반해 메모리 판가가 산업 평균을 재
차 능가하며 견조한 실적 개선이 가능했다. 
동사 메모리 사업부의 3Q26 영업이익은 109조원으로 예상되며, 이중 DRAM과 
NAND는 각각 80조원 (OPM 78.5%) 과 29조원 (OPM 68.9%) 을 차지할 전망이다. 
DRAM과 NAND 출하량은 기존 예상치를 소폭 하회하는 +3.4%/+4.2% QoQ을 기
록하겠지만, 판가가 무려 +20.1%/19.0% QoQ 상승하며 실적 개선을 이끌겠다. 전
분기에 이어 ‘DS 특별경영성과급’ 충당금이 반영되며 손익에 일부 영향을 미치겠지
만, 동사 특유의 ‘수익성 극대화’ 판가 정책이 산업 우위 구도를 조성한다. 한편 LSI/
파운드리는 영업손실이 1.5조원을 기록하며 적자가 지속될 전망이다. 가동률 개선에 
기반한 사업 회복이 진행중이지만, 테일러팹 가동과 함께 비용 요인이 지속 출회할 
전망이다. 한편 반도체 외 기타 사업부의 험난한 실적 추이는 지속될 전망이다. SDC 
1.1조원 영업이익을 제외하고, DX (세트) 부문의 경우 MX -1.9조원, DA/VD -7,400
억원으로 영업적자 확대되리라 예상된다. 당사가 지난해 말부터 주장했듯, 세트 사업
은 메모리 등 부품원가 비중이 급격히 상승하는 가운데 향후 ‘판가 인상 → 판매량 
축소’의 ‘내구재화’ 변모 속 적자 최소화 노력만이 불가피할 전망이다.

1Q27 일부 모바일 DRAM 판가 +50% 폭등 예상. 적극적 매수 권고 
AGI를 향한 경쟁적이며 구조적 서버 수요 증가 불구, 스마트폰 중심의 B2C 응용처
는 유일한 판가저항처로 남아있다. 하지만 당사의 채널체크에 따르면, 메모리 업체들
의 지속적인 생산 전환 (LPDDR → 서버 DRAM 및 HBM)에 기반해 주요 고객 향 
모바일 메모리 판가는 무려 1Q27부터 50% 이상 QoQ 상향될 전망이다. 주요 고객
은 내년 20주년 플래그십 스마트폰을 제작하는 상황에서 판가 폭등을 감내해야하는 
상황이다. 최근 메모리 사이클 개선 속도에 대한 막연하며 진부한 우려가 존재하나, 
향후 1~2개월간 메모리 업체들에 대한 2027년 실적 추정치 상향과 함께 ‘성장·저평
가·주주환원 매력’이 동시에 발생할 전망이다. 동사의 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 기
존의 562조원에서 632조원으로 13% 상향조정하며, 시장 눈높이 역시 지속 상향되
리라 예상한다. 한편 배당 중심으로 발표됐던 동사의 주주환원의 경우, 모든 주주에게 
더 실효적인 ‘자사주 매입·소각’ 방안이 병행되는 방향으로 변모하리라 예상된다. 투
자의견 Buy와 적정주가 50만원을 유지한다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 7

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 500,000원 
현재주가 (10.6) 272,000원 
상승여력 83.8%

MACRO STRATEGY
Setting the Stage for 2027
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
Typically, a new administration sets the stage in their first year with stimulative policy
changes that play out in the second year after the new policies have been enacted into
law and begin to have their impact. This pattern shows up in historical data with the
average rate of gross domestic product (GDP) growth highest in the second year of

presidential terms compared to the other three years of the presidential term. At the
same time, equity market returns tend to be lowest on average in the second year of
presidential terms, which seems to be at odds with the fact that growth is strongest.
This apparent inconsistency can be attributed to the fact that the stock market is a
leading indicator that anticipates stronger growth and higher profits well before they
actually arrive. Once they do, as they have in 2026, the markets are looking ahead to the

inevitable slowing from the policy-induced peak in growth that typically arrives after the
midterm election year. The midterms also create uncertainty about the subsequent policy
environment which damps stocks ahead of the election only to be reversed on average
once the elections are over and the outlook is clarified. This phenomenon is seen in the
fact that on average the stock market has corrected about 20% ahead of midterms only
to rise about 30% from that bottom in the year after.

Q3 GDP data illustrate this pattern. Economic growth picked up through the first three
quarters of 2026 to a peak of 3.4%. Consumer spending growth averaged 3.8% over the
past six months, while the AI investment boom accelerated at a midteens growth rate.
Private sector purchases were growing at just shy of a 5% growth rate. GDP growth is
lower than that because imports of Asia produced technology-gear for the AI buildout
are subtracting a good bit from that underlying 5% domestic demand growth pace.

The new policies aimed at helping consumers through lower taxes and investment
incentives to spur private sector production have boosted growth as expected. Still, this
acceleration is passing, and the fiscal boost is likely to fade in 2027. Profits naturally
have followed this stronger GDP growth higher.
The upward trend in analysts' earnings revisions has started to roll over in our view, and,
while earnings are still growing, the rate of growth is likely to slow from here. In simple

terms, the second derivative of growth has turned negative. From way above trend
toward trend growth is still sufficient for the bull market to continue.
Another aspect of this transition to midcycle aside from the fading midterm
election boost from fiscal measures is the shift of monetary policy from maximum
accommodation to a more neutral policy. The Fed's rate cuts helped the midterm
acceleration in growth. Its shift to removing accommodation with rate hikes this year

adds to the odds of some momentum loss in 2027. That's already apparent as Federal
Reserve (Fed) Chair Warsh noted in his press conference after the September Federal
Open Market Committee meeting. Weaker balance sheet borrowers have seen their
spreads blow out as credit becomes more expensive. So far, the damage is confined to
the lowest-quality borrowers. Before, these spreads were unusually low reflecting easy
money conditions that are now normalizing. It's still early days in the new credit cycle.

Of course, how the market responds to this new Fed tightening cycle depends on how
aggressively the Fed raises rates. Analysts at Ned Davis Research note the S&P 500
has gained an average of 4.4% in the first year of all postwar tightening cycles. This
outcome varies considerably depending on the extent of the tightening. Aggressive
tightening cycles have seen an average decline of 3.6% a year later, whereas gradual
tightenings have seen a 10.5% average gain. In short, as one might expect, too much

tightening can cause a decline in stocks. Currently, the market seems to anticipate a
Investment Implications
Equities should continue to outperform
bonds in 2027 in our view, but
performance is likely to shift in favor of
higher-quality, less leveraged segments
of the market as financial conditions
tighten and earnings momentum slows,
while the accelerating momentum of
the early cycle transitions to midcycle
cruise control.

### Page 3

3 of 8 November 9, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
December and March nudge up in rates to reverse the easings by the Powell Fed. This
would be consistent with an ongoing equity bull market in 2027. Obviously, inflation
will have a lot to say about whether a mild or aggressive tightening cycle is called for.
With wage pressures benign, productivity strong and the early-cycle acceleration set to
peak, we believe the odds of an aggressive tightening cycle that derails the bull market

are low.
Other policy effects have shaped the current economic cycle. The shift toward more
nationalistic trade policies with tariffs, for example, has drastically impacted the pattern
of trade flows. U.S. imports from China have dropped from about 20% 10 years ago to
less than 10%, according to research from Strategas. Nevertheless, contrary to concerns
of a trade-induced recession, overall trade has continued to grow in line with the global
economy. Rather than deglobalization, the world is undergoing a reglobalization into new

trade spheres that better align with national interests.
According to McKinsey Quarterly, "The 2025 U.S. National Security Strategy, for
example, reinforces economic and supply chain security, industrial capacity, technological
leadership, and capital market strength as national priorities. China's latest five-year plan,
meanwhile, includes technological self-reliance, economic stability and the modernization
of core industries among its key pillars. In this environment, value creation is increasingly

shaped by new trade corridors, growing defense spending, expansion of export controls
and industrial policies, and shifting flows of foreign investment." 1 These new trade
policies are an impetus for growth that is structural, helping to make for a strong
investment cycle.
Obviously, AI is a big spur for investment that has been facilitated by the new policies
such as accelerated depreciation of capital expenditures (capex). McKinsey Research
designates 18 arenas with cutting-edge industries they believe are driving the global

growth story for the next couple of decades. They note companies headquartered in
the U.S. and China account for about 90% of these arenas' market value today. U.S.
companies account for 75% of the market capitalization within the 18 arenas and
generate about 50% of revenues. Given its leading role in the arenas driving growth
into the future, we believe the U.S. is well poised to outperform as the world moves
into this new technological future. The 18 arenas are part of the economy growing by

several multiples of the old economy segments and help explain why U.S. Equities have a
persistent valuation premium over the rest of the world.
Because of the unusually rapid growth of these new technologies associated with the
singularity, while earnings growth may decelerate from its peak, there are structural
reasons to believe earnings are on a new, higher-growth trajectory and unlikely to revert
fully back to historical norms. The new technologies have much higher margins than the

old economy sectors. The combination of faster growth and higher margins bodes well
for the equity market as these new arenas of future economic growth become a bigger
part of the U.S. and global economies.
The midterms have changed views about the relative performance of some segments
of the economy. For example, strong sentiment to slow the buildout of AI data centers
was dramatically evident in the election results. Beneficiaries of the buildout are being

revalued to reflect this slower growth. Disapproval of the Iran War was also registered
in the results. Energy prices have dropped on hopes for a quicker end to the war. This in
turn has lowered inflation expectations and interest rates. Overall, while the midterms
have remixed relative performance between sectors they have not changed the outlook
for continued solid growth and an ongoing bull market in equities.
1 McKinsey Quarterly, Q3 2026, page 52.

### Page 4

4 of 8 November 9, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
China, the WTO and the Great Convergence No One Expected
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Twenty-five years ago this December, the West made one of the biggest economic bets
of the modern era: It brought China into the WTO, convinced that membership would
transform the communist giant into a more open and market-oriented economy.
The logic seemed impeccable at the time. The Soviet Union had long been vanquished.

American capitalism appeared triumphant. Free trade, deregulation, privatization and
unfettered cross-border flows of goods, services, capital and labor were widely viewed as
essential ingredients of economic prosperity. Globalization ruled.
Fold China into the rules-based system, the thinking went, and economic liberalization
would gradually pull Beijing toward Western norms. China would become a willing
participant in the U.S.-led global economic order. The world would change China, in

other words.
Not quite. At the dawn of the 25th anniversary of China’s WTO accession, the great irony is
not how China became more like the West. It is how the West became more like China.
In the U.S., and across Europe and Asia, governments are increasingly embracing the
elements of China’s economic playbook, which range from state subsidies to the promotion
of national champions to strategic industrial policies, among other initiatives antithetical to

the global economic norms that prevailed when China joined the WTO.
The deployment of trade tariffs, the subsidization of semiconductor production, the
promotion of domestic manufacturing, and the screening of foreign investment are no
longer hallmarks of China alone. They are increasingly features of economic policymaking
in the U.S., the one-time champion of globalization. The European Union, Japan, South
Korea and other nations that once carried the flag for free trade and open markets have

moved much in the same direction. Even the World Bank—a chief architect and guardian of
Western-led globalization—has come around to blessing the merits of industrial policy, a
strategy central to China’s economic ascent.
To be sure, Washington has not embraced China’s economic model wholesale. Neither have
Brussels, Tokyo or Seoul. The political, economic and institutional differences between
these systems and China’s remain vast. But the direction—set by China—is unmistakable.

It was not supposed to be this way. To gain admission to the WTO, China made significant
concessions over 15 years of negotiations. Beijing slashed tariffs on industrial goods and
agricultural products. Various sectors—manufacturing, distribution, telecommunications,
banking and autos—were gradually opened to foreign firms. Commercial laws and regulations
were amended or written, including rules governing intellectual property rights. Nontariff
barriers like quotas, import licenses, dual pricing and opaque industry standards were either

eliminated or phased out. State-owned enterprises were reformed and restructured.
These concessions—and more—were taken as signs that China was serious about
becoming a global stakeholder. It stoked optimism among senior U.S. officials that China
was inevitably bound to converge and embrace Western economic norms. And, for a while,
the bet seemed to pay off.
As China lowered trade and investment barriers at the beginning of the century, U.S. goods

exports soared. American firms poured capital into the mainland, attracted by an enormous
labor pool, lower production costs and what would soon become one of the largest
consumer markets in the world.
China’s massive labor pool-cum-ballooning middle class paid off handsomely for Corporate
America. U.S. affiliate income earned in China rose from just $1.6 billion (B) in 2001 to
nearly $9B by 2010, making China one of the most profitable markets in the world for U.S.

goods and services. American consumers, meanwhile, enjoyed the benefits of low-cost
China imports. And debt-laden America benefited as China recycled its excess savings into
U.S. Treasurys, helping to grease the financial wheels of America.
Investment Implications
We expect China will continue to rely
on exports and investment to drive
growth amid weak consumption and
domestic demand. Manufacturing-
heavy economies like Germany
remain particularly exposed,

reinforcing our neutral stance toward
Europe in portfolios.

### Page 5

5 of 8 November 9, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
But just as WTO accession helped boost China’s imports, it also supercharged China’s
export machine. Rather than shrink, as some optimists had predicted, America’s
merchandise trade deficit with China more than doubled between 2001 ($83.2B) and 2005
($202.8B) and continued rising thereafter. As the decade wore on, U.S. manufacturing jobs
and wages came under intense pressure owing to expanding China imports and the shift

in U.S. production to lower-cost China. Entire supply chains migrated across the Pacific,
decimating many manufacturing communities across the U.S.
China began producing and exporting at a scale the world had never experienced. In the
span of just a decade, China’s share of world manufacturing output soared from 8.7%
in 2000 to 18.1% in 2010, when the nation eclipsed the U.S. as the world’s largest
manufacturer of goods. Four years later, China was outproducing all of Europe—and has

never looked back (Exhibit 1A).
Today, China sits at the center of global manufacturing and trade, competing not simply in
textiles, toys and other low-value products but also in more technology-driven industries
like electric vehicles, drones, machinery and clean energy. China now accounts for roughly
28% of global manufacturing, according to the World Bank, well ahead of second place
America (17%). Meanwhile, the nation’s share of world exports—just 4% in 2001—now

stands at 15%, roughly on par with the combined market share of second-place America
and Germany (Exhibit 1B). The world is so awash with China goods that the nation is on
course to post another annual merchandise trade surplus in excess of $1 trillion.
Not surprisingly, China’s grip on world trade has significantly dimmed the brimming
optimism that greeted the nation’s WTO entry a quarter-century ago. Today, the consensus
is that China’s trade surplus has been aided and abetted by nonmarket practices and

policies that include forced technology transfers, currency manipulations, state subsidies
to industry, restrictions on foreign investment in strategic sectors, intellectual property
infringements and resource protectionism. Rather than dismantle its state-led, nonmarket
and mercantilist economy, China has tacked in the other direction, notably under the
leadership of Xi Jinping.
For investors, the implications have been profound. We now live in a world where

governments increasingly decide which industries are strategically indispensable, which
technologies must remain domestically controlled and which companies require public
financing. The “Commanding Heights”—or who controls the economy—have shifted
from the private sector to the public sector. In this world, investment returns are not only
determined by traditional metrics like earnings, the cost of capital and valuations but also
by geopolitics, energy costs, trade protectionism and national security. The world today is

far different from the world when China joined the WTO.
Suffice it to say that the 25th anniversary of China’s entry into the WTO won’t be met
with much celebration in the West. The consensus in 2001 was that the world would
change China. Twenty-five years later, it’s China that has changed the world. It’s the great
convergence no one saw coming.

### Page 6

6 of 8 November 9, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What are we reviewing to gauge the capital investment needed for the proposed Artificial
Intelligence buildout and the return required to cover its costs?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Officer
Capex by the 15 largest data center developers are expected to top $1.4 trillion in
2027, which is somewhere between 3.5% and 4% of U.S. GDP . A large portion of the
capital investment is earmarked for compute. The total capacity of gigawatts (GW)

under construction is around 34GW of Informaton Technology capacity, with the U.S.
accounting for approximately 70% of this, according to BloombergNEF. Given the
massive compute investment, we continue to analyze closely the total debt financing
announced, the approximate cost of capital of the capital providers, the associated
revenues needed to match or exceed cost estimates, and the individual credit spreads
(as well as credit default swaps spreads) of the larger hyperscalers and chip makers to

better assess the overall return on investment of the AI buildout.
BloombergNEF estimates, that at the beginning of this year, the highest-rated data
center bonds offered yields below 5%, which is now closer to around 7% today. In
addition, AI-tied firms have raised over $460B in debt financing this year, which is
more than double that from 2025. Finally, according to BloombergNEF and Bloomberg
Intelligence, as of September 18, 2026, it is estimated that the 15 largest publicly listed

data center companies would need to produce $2.5 trillion in revenues in 2032 in order
to achieve a return on invested capital equal to the company-level weighted average cost
of capital. This is about 5 times more than the expected revenue in 2026.
Recently, the concern across the capital markets is that there is not enough financing
capital to go around in order to fund the expected buildout, which is one of the primary
reasons why credit spreads have widened and yields have risen. We view this as a natural

development of such large-scale infrastructure transformations. This is the reason why
we continue to watch the overall health of balance sheets of the builders, the level of
profitability and margins, and the order backlogs/cancellations in the next few years. We
still believe we are closer to the middle of the AI buildout than to the end. Therefore, we
view larger-scale weakness in the higher-quality segments of the AI chain as potential
buying opportunities for investors who are underexposed to these areas.

prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.

Coming Out Of AI Bubble
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 8 Nov 2026

### Page 2

The AI selloff went global last week. In the US, Democrats swept
Congress in the midterm elections on Tuesday, November 3, surfing on a
huge wave of voter hostility toward new data centers. On Thursday,
investors dumped the AI trade worldwide, and Asia’s chipmakers took the
brunt of the wipeout. Overseas earnings are still climbing, but valuations
keep falling. We see the AI backlash as a speed bump, not a roadblock, at
home and abroad. Here’s more:

I. Stock Market Performance
Global sector leadership flipped this week according to the FTSE indexes
(chart). Defensive sectors held up best, with Real Estate up 2.2% and
Consumer Staples up 0.7%, while Consumer Discretionary led with a
2.7% gain. Technology (-6.7%) and Telecommunications (-7.4%) were
near the bottom. Energy was the worst performer, down 10.4%, as fears
of a post-midterm re-escalation in the Middle East faded. The FTSE World
index fell 2.5%.

The US has been gaining ground since we lowered our "Go Global"
investment strategy to market weight on October 18. The US MSCI’s
share of the All Country World MSCI’s market cap has climbed to 65.3%,
up from 62.4% in March (chart). The gains have come at the expense of
everyone else. The Emerging Markets MSCI’s share has dropped to
11.1% from 12.1% in mid-October.

### Page 3

The AI-heavy markets were at the bottom of the country ETF rankings this
week (chart). Taiwan fell 11.7% in dollars, South Korea 10.4%, and Japan
5.0%. This year’s laggards caught bids. South Africa led with an 8.5%
gain, followed by India at 6.6%. The US ETF fell 2.7%, slightly worse than
the All Country World ex-US ETF’s 1.8% decline.
Both the US and overseas ETFs have pulled back from their recent highs
(chart). Both remain in solid uptrends that began in late 2022.

### Page 4

II. Stock Market Earnings & Valuation
The All Country World ex-US MSCI’s forward earnings rose to yet another
record high this week (chart). It has soared since 2025 after going
nowhere for most of the previous decade.
Forward earnings is up 41.0% y/y, while forward revenues is up 11.2%
(chart). During solid economic expansions, forward revenue growth tends
to hover around 5%. Today’s double-digit pace confirms an economic
boom underpinning the global bull market.

### Page 5

Valuations continue to fall even as earnings soar (chart). The US MSCI’s
forward P/E is down to 18.9. Japan’s has dropped to 14.2 from 14.7 a
week ago. The All Country World ex-US trades at 12.0. The Emerging
Markets MSCI has slipped to single digits and is at 8.6 now.
III. Bonds
Sovereign bonds continued to rally worldwide this week as investors fled
the AI trade (chart). The US 10-year Treasury yield fell to 4.86%. The UK's
yield remains the highest in the group, at 5.16%, followed by Australia’s

5.00%. France’s yield has dropped to 4.77%, back below the US yield.
Japan’s yield is down to 2.63%.

### Page 6

IV. Focus on South Korea & Taiwan
South Korea's forward earnings is still rising to record highs, though more
slowly than earlier in the year (chart). The stock market isn’t buying it. The
MSCI South Korea stock price index is down more than 45% from its
record high. Its forward P/E has collapsed to 3.4, the lowest in over 20
years. Investors are betting that analysts will have to slash their earnings
estimates.
Taiwan tells the same story, but with a more sensible valuation. Its forward

earnings is also at a record high. Its forward P/E has dropped to 14.4 from
23.2 earlier this year (chart).

### Page 7

Fundamental data suggest South Korea and Taiwan should continue to
benefit from US demand for technology hardware. US imports of selected
technologies rose to a record $61.3 billion in September (chart).
Asia supplies much of that hardware. US imports from Vietnam rose to
$28.7 billion in September, and imports from Taiwan rose to $28.4 billion
(chart). Imports from South Korea rose to $19.2 billion. A slower US data
center buildout would delay that demand, not derail it. We would view a

slower pace as more sustainable over a longer period of time.

### Page 8

clause.
Yardeni QuickTakes © 2026 – Unsubscribe

Our base case assumes solid growth, cooling inﬂation and the
Fed hiking once more as Middle East tensions subside. However,
if President Trump feels constrained after the midterm
elections and hawks within the Administration push for more
aggression on Iran, we will face a very diﬀerent economic

James Knightley
AI investment could supercharge US economic growth
How to read our three calls for 2027

Source: ING

### Page 2

ING's base call: Solid growth and
cooling inﬂation remains our call
We continue to expect respectable US growth of 2-2.5% over
the next couple of years, but with ongoing bifurcation.
Wealth gains are supporting spending among higher-income
households, while weaker wage growth is pressuring middle-
and lower-income households.
Within business investment, tech-related capex shows little
sign of slowing, but appears to be cannibalising investment
spending elsewhere and is contributing to the low-hire, low-

ﬁre economy. Manufacturing is similarly K-shaped: high-
value-added advanced manufacturing is growing strongly
and adding jobs, while more commoditised, labour-intensive
manufacturing struggles. The growth story's lack of breadth
is a slight concern and creates vulnerabilities, particularly if
asset prices correct.
Inﬂation remains above target but should ease as energy
costs fall and rents cool. More signiﬁcantly, labour costs are
the biggest cost input for corporate America. With wage

growth dropping to 3%, we see just one more Fed rate rise –
in December – before a long pause for most of 2027.
Our risky call: Constrained Trump goes all-in
for regime change in Iran
With President Trump constrained by a Democratic-led Congress,
foreign and trade policy remain key sources of risk to our base case.
For example, Iran hawks within the Administration could convince
him that diplomacy won't work, meaning regime change in Iran is
required. Disruption in the Gulf would then intensify, with energy

prices spiking. At the same time, President Trump could demand
NATO involvement, applying onerous tariﬀs on imports from
members that refuse to participate in the conﬂict with Iran.

In this scenario, rising inﬂation could prompt the Fed to raise rates
above 5%. Government borrowing would rise on military spending,
but Treasury yields would be held down by 'safe-haven' buying as
equity markets plunge. Recession risks would then mount through

demand destruction as unemployment climbs. Iran would
eventually be weakened suﬃciently that attacks cease and energy
ﬂows resume. Oil, natural gas and fertiliser prices would then
subside, with inﬂation falling rapidly. Monetary policy would be
eased aggressively, but it would be too little, too late to prevent a
painful downturn for the global economy.
Our bold call: Party like it's 1999? US
growth hits 5% in 2027
US GDP growth averaged 4.5% in the four years before the dotcom

crash in 2000. Yet for all the talk of the surge in AI-related
investment spending in the current cycle, the economy has only
averaged 2.7% annual growth since ChatGPT launched four years
ago. In our bold call, that would change in 2027.
In this scenario, AI unleashes a wave of productivity improvements
and technological advancement across sectors more rapidly than
currently envisaged. Prices fall and real wages surge. Rather than
using the productivity gains to justify job cuts, companies reorient

their workforce to do much more. Tax revenues surge while AI
adoption in government creates huge eﬃciency savings and a re-
evaluation of the ﬁscal outlook. Lower deﬁcits, lower debt
projections and lower inﬂation prompt Treasury yields to plunge.
The Fed cuts rates while mortgage rates drop below 4%, triggering
a recovery in the residential property market. The economy booms
broadly, with equity markets soaring to new highs.

## Document 418: The FOMC pause to reflect (2026-11-11)

Source PDF: all report pdf/The FOMC pause to reflect (2026-11-11).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#fomc #usd #us
The FOMC pause to reflect
By Elliot Clarke
Head of International Economics, Westpac
08:14 October 29 2026 Share
No change in October, though confidence in the economy's strength and two dissents point
to a hike in December.
The FOMC held at their October meeting, albeit with two dissents, Governor Cook and Cleveland Fed
President Hammack preferring to hike by 25bps at this meeting. The statement was short and, in the
press conference, Chair Warsh again declined to provide definitive guidance on the near-term policy

### Page 2

Related articles
outlook.
The Committee remains positive on economic activity, characterising it in the statement as “expanding
at a strong pace” (an upgrade from “solid” in September). Despite elevated uncertainty, “domestic
spending has been resilient”, underpinned by “strong” productivity growth and “robust” capital
investment. Chair Warsh subsequently also emphasised the breadth of economic growth outside of
tech-related investment. Labour demand and supply were represented as balanced and resilient in

both the statement and press conference.
The Committee refrained from spotlighting key price risks in the statement. However, inflation “remains
elevated” and the FOMC are determined to achieve a “timely” and sustainable return of annual
consumer inflation to 2.0%yr after 6 years above target.
Along with the two dissents, this guidance and the “accumulation of more information will help the
Committee determine the policy stance” suggests another 25bp hike is likely in December. Thereafter,

the FOMC will remain attentive to any broadening in inflation and related risks through 2027.
Towards the end of the press conference, Chair Warsh also announced a structural change in the
FOMC schedule. While there will still be 8 meetings in 2027, a meeting in the fall will focus on
monetary policy strategy versus the current stance of monetary policy. Chair Warsh then intends for 2
of the 8 meetings in 2028 to have this strategy focus. If realised, this will see the FOMC review and

vote on the current stance of monetary policy just 6 times per calendar year from 2028.

《投資關鍵 – 澳洲景氣放緩、澳央轉向觀望，預期
澳幣盤整》
隨著國際油價維持高位，澳洲央行（RBA）於9月再
度升息將現金利率提高至 4.6%，官員雖表示若通膨
壓力持續，不排除進一步緊縮。
但隨著高利率與高通膨壓力持續拖累經濟動能，近期
公布的 9月就業意外減少 3.93萬人 、失業率更升至
4.7%，已連續兩個月落在澳央主席認為可有效壓抑
通膨的失業率4.5~5.0%區間；通膨方面，9月份澳洲
CPI雖由4.0%升至4.3%，但核心CPI僅有2.2%，加以
住房通膨明顯回落，因此本行預期澳央轉向觀望、本
輪升息暫時告終 ，2027年政策轉向觀望，如國際油
價回落至90美元以下，澳央有降息空間。
外需方面，中國經濟基本面持續低迷，消費與投資不

振亦難以挹注澳洲出口動能，外需對澳幣支撐力量有
限，加以預期聯準會在 12月再度升息 ，將呈現「利
差有撐但優勢遞減」格局，因此預估澳幣短線維持在
0.7~0.71區間盤整，要進一步突破或要待2Q27美國
通膨回落、降息預期再度升溫，方有走揚力量。

紐西蘭通膨升溫訊號浮現，月度物價年增 5.2%
《投資關鍵 - 央行意外按兵不動拖累紐元 ，後續升息
預期提供下檔支撐》
過去一個月 ，受美元強勢 、美債殖利率及油價走高拖
累，紐幣兌美元一度下探 0.5565，其後隨升息預期升
溫而回穩 。惟紐西蘭央行 10月28日意外維持 OCR於
2.75%不變，促使市場下修短期升息預期 ，紐幣再度
承壓。
紐西蘭經濟延續溫和復甦 ，企業信心及農產品價格改
善，惟勞動市場仍有閒置產能 。雖然整體通膨低於預
期，但核心通膨及價格廣度上升 ，加上油價高企與紐

幣偏弱，我們預期4Q26通膨仍具黏性，價格壓力擴散
風險猶存。
我們仍維持偏鷹派的政策基準情境 。10月以4比3票按
兵不動，較反映升息時點後移 ，而非整體政策路徑轉
向，緊縮週期尚未結束。我們仍預期12月及明年3月具
升息空間 ，尤其若通膨 、油價及匯率壓力持續 ，政策
立場料維持偏緊。
展望4Q26，10月意外按兵不動削弱紐幣利差支撐，我
們仍看好紐元中期修復空間 ，惟短期上行動能有所減
弱。升息預期降溫 ，加上大選 、美元及全球風險情緒
等不確定性，料使匯價維持較高波動。進入1Q27後，
隨政策不確定性逐步下降 ，經濟溫和復甦有望成為紐
幣主要支撐 。考量匯價已反映相當程度的成長疑慮及
外部利空 ，若基本面持續改善 ，紐幣中期仍具修復空
間。

通膨數據回落，6月降息的可能性加大重點摘要 《投資關鍵 - 央行轉鷹支撐加幣，惟經濟及貿易風險
限制升值空間》
能源價格高企令加拿大通膨面臨上行風險，加拿大央
行政策預期轉鷹亦推動加美利差劣勢收窄，支持加幣
自10月中旬低點反彈 。不過，隨本次25個基點升息
已大致反映於市場定價，加幣能否延續升勢將更取決
於央行對後續緊縮路徑的指引，以及市場對終端利率
的重新定價，而非本次升息本身。
加拿大央行最新升息 25個基點至2.5%，為逾三年來
首次升息，並表示若能源價格進一步推升廣泛通膨壓
力，不排除繼續收緊政策 。央行亦將 2027年通膨預
測由 2.0%上調至 3.1%，並將今年經濟成長預測由
0.7%上調至1.0%，短期政策訊號偏鷹 。惟核心通膨
仍相對溫和，勞動市場、製造業需求及招聘意願亦呈
轉弱跡象，意味若經濟活動進一步降溫，持續升息空
間或有限。高油價及偏高的投機性加幣空頭部位仍構
成短期利好，但美加貿易不確定性可能繼續拖累企業
投資及成長前景。因此，我們維持4Q26至1Q27加幣

中性看法。短期加美利差收窄及油價高企有望限制加
幣下行風險，但在增長前景改善或央行釋出更明確的
持續緊縮訊號前，加幣進一步升值空間有限。

，6月降息的可能性加大重點摘要
• 從景氣趨勢、政策取向與國
際經貿局勢變化三大視角切

《投資關鍵 – 政經拉鋸與景氣K型下，人民幣溫和升值》
中國公布最新 3Q26 GDP成長4.5%，出口年增率升至
28.4%，貿易順差由前季的3148.6億美元擴大至3651.2
億美元 。市場預估中國 2026年全年貿易順差將超越
2025年1.2兆美元紀錄，再創新高。惟中國外需繁榮卻
無法掩蓋內需疲軟困境 ，中國3Q26零售銷售年增率僅
0.7%，固定資產投資衰退7%。儘管中國陸續推出穩增
長政策，大多以財政政策為主軸，貨幣政策偏向定向式
而非全面降準降息。因此儘管美啟動升息，中美利差尚
保持穩定，因此內需疲軟與有限度的寬鬆貨幣政策對人
民幣走勢並未形成顯著壓力。
中國的出口暢旺除受惠科技週期貢獻外，強勁汽車出口
至歐洲也是主因，9月中國汽車出口維持62%的高成長。
惟加劇保護主義傾向，中歐達成初步協議，未來四年內
將對歐洲的混合動力車出口量減少一半以上，具體配額、

最低售價及豁免條件仍待協商。政策角度觀察，人民幣
升值本身可能成為中國應對外部壓力的重要工具，減緩
低估匯率促進出口的國際批評。
考量關稅可能成為攻防戰手段之一，研判中國廠商或搶
出口支持外貿表現高檔，同時官方可能對人民幣升值空
間容忍態度提高 ，疊加前三季結售匯順差高達 3,941億
美元，創2010年以來新高，顯示企業結匯意願強勁，持
續為人民幣提供支撐。從景氣趨勢、政策取向與國際經
貿局勢變化的三大視角切入，綜合判斷人民幣溫和升值
的看法不變。

### Page 6

《投資關鍵 - 基本面改善但部位趨擁擠，南非幣料維
持震盪》
南非幣第四季以來震盪偏弱，USD/ZAR近期回升至約
16.60。外部環境仍偏不利，高油價推升進口成本與
通膨壓力，加上美元偏強及美國南非利差收窄，削弱
南非幣套息吸引力。與此同時，巴西總統大選結果引
發市場波動，增添新興市場不確定性，短期投資人風
險偏好轉趨審慎，亦可能加重新興市場貨幣的資金流
出壓力。
基本面方面，南非財政紀律改善、主權信用前景轉佳，
加上較高實質利率及具吸引力的利差，仍為南非幣提
供支撐。惟高油價及聖嬰現象相關極端天氣風險令通
膨壓力偏高，限制政策寬鬆空間。另一方面，近期投
機性及資產管理機構多頭部位明顯增加，顯示市場部
位漸趨擁擠。在外部風險升溫及風險偏好轉弱下，獲
利了結與部位調整可能放大短期波動，即使匯價回檔，
逢低布局的風險報酬吸引力仍相對有限。
整體而言，南非幣基本面有所改善，但短期外部環境
與部位面均偏不利。財政紀律改善、主權信用前景轉
佳及實質利率優勢有助建立匯率下檔支撐，惟強勢美
元、高油價、利差收窄及偏擁擠的多頭部位限制升值

空間。預期4Q26美元兌南非幣仍以區間震盪為主，維
持南非幣中性看法。
重點摘要
資料來源：CFTC，Bloomberg，2026/9，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10

## Document 486: 【趨勢主題】歐洲市場問答：高利環境下的壓力與機會

Source PDF: all report pdf/【趨勢主題】歐洲市場問答：高利環境下的壓力與機會.pdf
Report source: CTBC
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

時性和正確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之
需求及投資之風險，並就投資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不
涉及特定投資標的之建議。
歐洲市場問答：高利環境下的壓力與機會
1
Thematic Report
重點提示
中東地緣衝突自2026年2月底爆發以來仍陷僵局，能源價格維
持高檔的時間較預期更長，令歐元區通膨壓力與升息預期升溫。
另一方面，歐洲政府擴大財政支出，公債供給增加，加上歐洲
央行持續縮表，推升期限溢價，令歐洲公債殖利率大幅走升，
德國十年期公債殖利率9/28升破3.6%，創2009年6月以來新高。
本報告透過問答，分析歐洲公債殖利率走高的原因、主權債務
與財政風險、政府因應措施、高利率對企業的影響，以及高利
率環境下的歐洲股債展望。
◼ 本波歐洲公債殖利率走高的驅動因素：能源價格上漲推升通
膨、市場重估歐央貨幣政策路徑，同時，財政支出令歐洲公

債供給增加與歐央持續縮表使私人部門需要吸收更多公債供
給，推升期限溢價，財政疑慮使部分歐洲國家利差承受額外
壓力，加上全球利率上升的外溢影響，共同推升殖利率走高。
◼ 歐洲政府調整發債結構，央行備有穩市工具：歐洲政府縮短
發債期限以控制融資成本，預估此趨勢將延續至2027年；
萬一主權債市場出現無序波動，歐洲央行有OMT與TPI政策
工具，但不會輕易動用。
◼ 整體歐洲債務風險尚可控但中長期壓力持續累積：目前利率
水準尚未對整體債務可持續性構成普遍威脅，但財政脆弱性
較高的國家壓力升高，法國尤其需要關注。財政與政治變數
使法國資產風險溢價維持高位，目前股債定價已反映相當程
度的負面風險。
◼ 德國公債殖利率短期將維持高檔：歐洲央行12月仍有再升息
可能，待能源價格與通膨壓力緩和、升息預期降溫後，利率
才有回落空間，但難降至3%以下。
◼ 歐洲企業資產負債表健康、獲利具韌性，緩衝高利率衝擊。
◼ 高利率限制歐股估值擴張，歐股維持中立，聚焦獲利成長與
政策支撐的產業：銀行受惠於目前利率環境與信貸需求，工

業、電氣化/能源安全及AI受惠戰略自主與資本支出提升。
Sep. 29, 2026
林亞瑱
歐洲總經政策與市場分析

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/9/15，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/9。
能源價格大漲引發政策路徑重估為利率上揚主因
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/9/15，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/9。
德國公債殖利率曲線上移，短率升幅大於長率
中東衝突發生以來德國公債殖利率曲線(中東地緣衝突前 vs. 目前)
近期全球公債殖利率大幅上揚 ，歐洲國家亦然，德國10年期公
債殖利率9/28升破3.6%，創2009年6月來新高，若從2月底中

東地緣衝突爆發以來起算 ，德國十年債利率累積彈升了近 100
個基點，30年期德債利率亦逼近4%(至9/28止)。
Q1：什麼因素推動今年歐洲公債殖利率走高？
(1)能源價格上漲推升通膨、市場重估歐央貨幣政策路徑，為驅
動本波利率上揚的主因：
2月底中東衝突爆發以來 ，德國10年期公債殖利率與油氣價格
走勢高度相關。此外，德國公債各期限殖利率全面上移 ，其中
短率升幅高於長率 ，反映本波利率上揚主 要受到能源價格上升
所帶動的通膨風險，以及市場對ECB政策路徑重新定價的影響。
此外，美國殖利率上升透過全球資本市場產生外溢效果 ，帶動
全球無風險利率中樞上移 ，亦對歐洲公債殖利率形成上行壓力 。

歐洲政府發債規模增與ECB縮表同步推升債券供給
■淨債券發行 ■ECB縮表 ◆對私人部門的淨供給
(2)歐洲公債供給增加、央行持續縮表，推升期限溢價：
德國擴大財政支出，歐洲政府融資需求及公債供給增加；同時 ，
ECB持續進行資產負債表正常化 ，逐步減少公債持有量 ，使新
增債券供給需由私人投資人吸收 ，推升投資人要求的期限溢價 。
除此之外，政府債務持有者結構轉變 ，個人與海外投資人等對
價格較為敏感的投資人占比增加，使債市更容易受到市場情緒
與金融條件變化的影響。
(3)財政疑慮使部分歐洲國家利差承受額外壓力：
歐洲部分國家面臨較高的政府債務壓力，加上政治局勢限制財
政調整空間，市場對債務可持續性的疑慮難消 。尤其在年度預
算協商期間，財政脆弱性較高的國家如法國 、英國，主權債務

風險重新定價容易反映在長端殖利率及與德債的利差擴大。

涉及特定投資標的之建議。
Q2：面對公債殖利率上升，歐洲政府如何因應？
面對公債殖利率上升 ，歐洲政府調整發債結構 、增加短年期債
券發行，以控制高利率環境下的融資成本 ，預期此趨勢將延續
至2027年；惟縮短債務期限雖可降低當期融資成本，也將提高
中期再融資風險。
Q3：萬一歐洲主權債市場出現無序波動，歐洲有哪些
政策工具可以運用？
歐洲央行主要政策工具有二 ，分別為直接貨幣交易 (OMT)與傳
導保護工具(TPI)。
(1)直接貨幣交易(OMT)：2012年9月推出，源自歐債危機，可
在次級市場購買符合條件國家的1至3年期政府公債，且購買
規模事前不設上限 ，惟須搭配歐洲穩定機制 (ESM)的金融援
助計畫，並具嚴格的附帶條件，使用門檻較高，由主權國家

主動申請。
(2)傳導保護工具 (TPI)：2022年7月推出 ，可在次級市場購買
符合條件國家的 1至10年期政府部門債券 ，目的在於因應與
該國基本面不相稱的無序市場波動 ，避免金融市場分裂影響
貨幣政策傳導。啟動時將評估該國是否符合歐盟財政規範 、
債務是否具備可持續性等條件 ，由歐央評估後決定是否啟動。
不過，OMT與TPI均屬市場失序時的最後手段 ，並非用於壓低
正常的主權債殖利率或消除國家間的基本面利差 ，啟動有嚴格
條件，歐央不會輕易動用，目前此兩項工具均未實際動用過。
高利率下，歐洲政府調整發債、央行備有穩市工具

Q4：歐洲政府債務的可持續性是否有立即風險？
主要歐洲國家除德國與西班牙外 ，IMF預估2026年政府債務佔
GDP比重均超過 100%。同時，近年地緣衝突頻率升高 ，戰略
自主與強化地區安全已成為歐洲核心目標 。為了讓成員國有擴
大國防支出的空間 ，歐盟允許成員國啟動 國家豁免條款，得以
在增加國防支出的同時 ，暫時不受歐盟財政規則約束 ，截至目
前已有18個歐盟會員國申請。國防支出提升，若未搭配增加財
政收入或其他支出調整，將使政府債務維持在較高路徑。
所幸，從利率與經濟成長率差距來看 ，目前利率水準尚未對歐
洲整體債務可持續性構成 立即威脅，但部分高負債國家的債務
壓力仍須留意。過去十年低利率環境的效益逐步消退 ，利率與

經濟成長率的差距 (r－g)較過去不利 ，加上期限溢價回升 ，使
高負債國家的債務動態面臨風險。
檢視歐元區四大經濟體 ，德國、西班牙政府債務的利率低於經
濟成長率，債務動態相對穩健；義大利政府債務 佔GDP比重最
高，所幸在政府控制支出下，自2024年以來維持初級財政盈餘，
有助控制債務比率進一步擴大。法國利率雖略低於經濟成長率 ，
但政府債務已接近 GDP的120%，在財政赤字偏高 、利息支出
持續增加且財政整頓進展有限 的情況下，未來兩年債務佔GDP
比率預期將持續上升，財政脆弱性相對較高。
整體歐洲債務風險可控但中長期壓力正在累積

Q5：近期法德利差顯著擴大，驅動因素與對法國股債
市的影響？
財政與政治不確定性升溫 ，推升法國主權風險溢價 ：法國步入
財政與政治不確定性升溫時期 ，不利的財政前景 ，加上4Q26
預算協商與2027年將舉行總統大選，政治不確定性升溫推升十
年期法德利差9月下旬突破100個基點，9/28升至112個基點、
創2012年6月以來新高。法國總統大選將於2027年4~5月舉行，
4/18舉行第一輪投票 ，若無候選人過半 ，5/2將舉行第二輪投
票。由於法國政治版圖高度分裂 、且極右派支持度領先 ，相較
於過往選舉周期，2027年法國選舉的不確定更高，預估將令市
場在2027年總統大選前維持謹慎情緒。
民調反映政治版圖分裂 ，增加財政政策的不確定性： 民調顯示，
極右派國民聯盟候選人瑪琳·勒龐在總統大選首輪的支持度領先，
超過三成，極左派法國不屈黨候選人梅朗雄 、中間偏右候選人
愛德華·菲利普民調落在15~18%左右。法國大選高機率將會進
入第二輪投票，取決於進入第二輪的候選人 ，目前民調顯示以

中間偏右候選人菲利普最有希望擊敗勒龐 ，若為極左派梅朗雄
進入第二輪，則勒龐勝選機率較高。

極端政治勢力候選人在本次大選勝選的可能性提高 ，為本已脆
弱的法國財政增添另一層不確定性 。與以往政治週期不同 ，法
國進入2027年大選週期時，財政迴旋餘地相當有限，加上法國
